# Skills · P · part 85

862 entries, plan-first to plan-lookup. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **plan-first**: Use before any non-trivial code change — a refactor, new feature, migration, or multi-file edit. Investigate the affected code first, produce a concise implementation plan (files to change, approach, risks, test strategy), get the user's confirmation, then execute. Prevents premature edits and surprise blast radius on risky work. ([matthews-wong/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/matthews-wong/claude-code-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/plan-mode-helper/skills/plan-first) · ★3)
- **plan-first**: Decides whether a task needs a plan before any change, writes that plan and waits for your yes. It maps a new codebase, agrees the goal, numbers every file, marks the steps that are hard to undo, and splits a big refactor into steps you approve one at a time. Fixes "It started changing things before I knew what it was going to do." Use when one change touches many files, when the goal is vague ("make it faster"), when a step is hard to undo (deleting data, dropping a table, a deploy), when the agent starts in a codebase it has not read, or before a big refactor. Also on /plan-first. ([nadavnaveh/skills](https://github.com/nadavnaveh/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/plan-first) · ★3)
- **plan-first**: Produce and maintain an implementation plan for risky, ambiguous, irreversible, or coordinated changes, and whenever the user asks for a plan or approach. Trigger on public contracts, schemas, auth, payments, migrations, build or deployment systems, and material design choices. Do not use for low-risk mechanical work with an obvious established implementation. ([adityaarakeri/senior-agent-skills](https://github.com/adityaarakeri/senior-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/plan-first) · ★2)
- **plan-first**: Makes a short written plan with a definition of done before starting any multi-step task, and gets it confirmed when the task is costly to redo. Use when a task has several steps or files, is ambiguous, or the user says "спланируй", "составь план", "plan this", "how should we approach". ([dmminga1983/minga](https://github.com/dmminga1983/minga/tree/HEAD/plugins/minga/skills/plan-first) · ★2)
- **plan-first**: Front door for work that will span more than one file, review unit, or commit. Produces the plan before the first edit: the claim, the done-gate, the slice boundaries, the gates that will run, and the base it runs against. Names the invariants that apply rather than restating them. Use before authoring implementation work, or when a task is about to become a PR stack. ([EdbertChan/catstack](https://github.com/EdbertChan/catstack/tree/HEAD/product/skills/plan-first) · ★2)
- **plan-first**: Enforces making a structured plan before writing files or making changes. Prevents looping and unstructured debugging by requiring explicit steps. ([hikaru90/eigen](https://github.com/hikaru90/eigen/tree/HEAD/.pi/skills/plan-first) · ★2)
- **plan-first**: For tasks touching 3+ files or ambiguous scope — enumerate files, ≤5-line plan, confirm, execute without pausing. In plan mode on big or repetitive work, also write the plan for a cheaper executor model and verify its result. Trigger before multi-file features, refactors, redesigns, or tasks with two plausible interpretations. ([AftabIbrahimKazi/fish-pond](https://github.com/AftabIbrahimKazi/fish-pond/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/plan-first) · ★1)
- **plan-first**: 작업 시작 전 계획서·맥락노트·체크리스트 3문서를 먼저 만들고, 검토 후 실행하는 스킬. 건축의 설계도·시방서·공정표처럼 맥락을 잃지 않고 작업한다. "/plan", "/plan-first", "계획 세워줘", "작업 계획", "시작 전에 정리" 요청에 사용. ([eoash/ash-skills](https://github.com/eoash/ash-skills/tree/HEAD/plan-first) · ★1)
- **plan-first**: Use before a bigger build (a feature, portal, pipeline, analysis, new repo) when the request is large or underspecified, when choosing a stack or design, or when asked to align, scope or plan first. Interviews, then writes a spec and hands off to a fresh session. Not for small edits, bug fixes, or questions with an obvious answer. ([samuelbfernandes/moddeck](https://github.com/samuelbfernandes/moddeck/tree/HEAD/skills/plan-first) · ★1)
- **plan-first**: Before writing code for a feature or change that touches more than one or two files, briefly outline the approach first. Use when the user asks to build, add, refactor, or fix something non-trivial. ([0xcnr0/treats](https://github.com/0xcnr0/treats/tree/HEAD/skills/plan-first))
- **plan-first**: Turn a task goal into a standalone, human-readable HTML plan. Use `/plan-first` at the start of a workspace: if `project.md` exists, render it directly without re-interviewing; if it is missing, ask up to three short questions, confirm the goal, write `project.md`, then generate `plan.html`. The HTML is a reading view only and never replaces `project.md` as the source of truth. It writes no code, tests, dependencies or settings. 中文触发词：plan-first、任务目标可视化、生成计划书、项目计划 HTML、需求可视化。 ([2huy4n/plan-first](https://github.com/2huy4n/plan-first/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **plan-first**: Before implementing non-trivial or ambiguous work — a feature, a multi-file change, or a real design choice — scope a short plan and confirm the goal before coding. - **plan-first**: Write a short step-by-step implementation plan and get it accepted before changing code. Use once what to build is clear, for any change that touches more than one source file, needs more than about three distinct edits, adds a dependency, or changes a data schema or public interface; also when the user asks to plan, outline an approach, or "think before coding". Skip for small single-purpose edits confined to one file (typos, version bumps, one-function fixes), even if a lockfile changes too. If scope is still open, use idea-to-spec first. ([gala-ud/skills](https://github.com/gala-ud/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/plan-first))
